They explain business-performance movement—not billing findings or verified savings.<\u002Fspan>\n                <\u002Fspan>\n                <span aria-hidden=\"true\" style=\"color:var(--imp-fg-subtle); font-size:11px; flex:none; margin-top:3px; transform:{{ bi.recMethChevron }};\">▾<\u002Fspan>\n              <\u002Fbutton>\n              <sc-if value=\"{{ bi.recMethOpen }}\" hint-placeholder-val=\"{{ false }}\">\n                <div style=\"display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:12px; padding:0 14px 14px 35px; animation:imp-fade-in 0.2s ease;\">\n                  <div>\n                    <div style=\"font:700 11px var(--imp-font-body); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:0.05em; color:var(--imp-fg-subtle); margin-bottom:3px;\">Reconciliation<\u002Fdiv>\n                    <p class=\"imp-small\" style=\"margin:0;\">Signed factor contributions reconcile to the total change of {{ bi.driverTotalArrow }} {{ bi.driverTotalAbs }} in cost per order between {{ bi.prevPeriod }} and {{ bi.period }}.<\u002Fp>\n                  <\u002Fdiv>\n                  <div>\n                    <div style=\"font:700 11px var(--imp-font-body); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:0.05em; color:var(--imp-fg-subtle); margin-bottom:3px;\">Method<\u002Fdiv>\n                    <p class=\"imp-small\" style=\"margin:0;\">Each factor is estimated by holding the other factors at their comparison-period values. This isolates the estimated contribution of each factor to the overall movement.<\u002Fp>\n                  <\u002Fdiv>\n                  <div>\n                    <div style=\"font:700 11px var(--imp-font-body); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:0.05em; color:var(--imp-fg-subtle); margin-bottom:3px;\">Drill-down behavior<\u002Fdiv>\n                    <p class=\"imp-small\" style=\"margin:0;\">Select a factor to review where the change was concentrated and the records or related analysis available to investigate it.
